Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geochemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 653. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 784 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to geochemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 653 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 784. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
